DYNAMICS LAB · OPERATIONS TWIN · SCIENTIFIC REPORT

OTE-001 · Enterprise Process Reconstruction

OBSERVED. Observed elapsed time, simulated service assumptions and business outcomes are distinct. This report does not assess individual employees or calculate monetary savings.

Objective and dataset

BPI Challenge2019 · public Purchase-to-Pay log · Boudewijn van Dongen · 4TU.ResearchData · version1 · CC BY4.0. Original input:728,558,522bytes, checked against official MD5.

Official dataset and attribution

Method and parameters

Full official BPI2019 XES is parsed using defusedxml streaming, with original MD5/size checked against4TU metadata. Required fields are validated; UTC timestamps are integer microseconds. Exact full-attribute duplicate candidates remain in the original analysis because no unique event identifier establishes erroneous duplication. Stable timestamp/source order constructs traces, category-specific DFG and variants. Durations are elapsed first-to-last observations, not processing/waiting time. A strict2018sensitivity excludes entire out-of-window cases separately. Repeated activities and Change/Cancel prefixes are descriptive proxies, not proven waste. No monetary costs or individual productivity are inferred.

Calculated figures

Uncertainty and limitations

  • Elapsed first-to-last observation is not processing time or waiting time. No start/completion lifecycle supplied in inspected source schema; full distribution audited.
  • Repeated goods receipts/invoices can be legitimate installments; repetition is not automatically waste or rework. Change/Cancel is an explicit descriptive proxy.
  • Declared2018coverage differs from any out-of-period observed timestamps; flagged observations remain in full discovery and a strict-window sensitivity is separate.
  • WIP is an observed interval proxy and last-observation counts are not independently verified true business completions.
  • Long elapsed transitions suggest investigation; employee productivity, causes and monetary savings are not inferred.
  • All model validation uses an explicitly projected, endpoint-proxy, quarter-contained and document-disjoint subset; not all251734cases.
